hemisphere from the CCF ML coordinate of the unit's electrode\n        el = f['general/extracellular_ephys/electrodes']\n        ex = el['x'][:]\n        e_of_unit = u['electrodes'][:][np.concatenate([[0], u['electrodes_index'][:][:-1]])]\n        ml = ex[e_of_unit]\n        region_names = []\n        for k in unit_ids:\n            grp = map_annotation(anno[k])\n            side = 'left' if (not np.isnan(ml[k]) and ml[k] >= ML_MIDLINE) else 'right'\n            region_names.append('%s %s' % (side, grp))\n\n        # ---------------- trials -----------------------------------------------------\n        t = f['intervals/trials']\n        trial_start = t['start_time'][:]\n        trial_stop = t['stop_time'][:]\n        outcome = _decode(t['outcome'][:])\n        early = _decode(t['early_lick'][:])\n        instruction = _decode(t['trial_instruction'][:])\n        auto_water = t['auto_water'][:] > 0\n        free_water = t['free_water'][:] > 0\n        ntrials_file = len(trial_start)\n\n        go_all = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times/timestamps'][:]\n        if len(go_all) != ntrials_file:\n            print('%s: %d go cues for %d trials, skipping' % (sess_id, len(go_all), ntrials_file),\n                  flush=True)\n            return None\n\n        # reference `get_regular_trial_mask` also removes auto/free water trials; early\n        # lick / no-response / photostim trials are decoder targets or inputs and kept\n        keep = ~(auto_water | free_water)\n        keep &= np.isfinite(go_all)\n\n        # In 8 of the 173 sessions the ephys recording covers only part of the\n        # behavioural session: the units' obs_intervals then span a contiguous subset\n        # of trials and the remaining trials contain no spikes at all.  Keep only\n        # trials that are inside the observation window of every good unit.\n        keep &= observed_trial_mask(u, unit_ids, trial_start, trial_stop)\n        trial_idx = np.where(keep)[0]\n        if len(trial_idx) < 2:\n            print('%s: <2 usable trials, skipping' % sess_id, flush=True)\n            return None\n\n        go = go_all[trial_idx]\n        edges_abs = go[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]\n        centers_abs = go[:, None] + BIN_CENTERS[None, :]\n\n        # ---------------- neural ------------------------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        spikes = u['spike_times'][:]\n        s0, s1 = _spike_slices(u['spike_times_index'][:])\n        fr = bin_spikes(spikes, s0, s1, unit_ids, edges_abs)     # (n_units, n_trials, NBINS)\n        del spikes\n\n        # A handful of trials sit at the very end of a recording: they are listed in\n        # obs_intervals but the probe was already switched off, so not a single spike\n        # was recorded in the extracted window.  Such trials carry no neural data.\n        has_spikes = fr.sum(axis=(0, 2)) > 0\n        if not has_spikes.all():\n            fr = fr[:, has_spikes, :]\n            trial_idx = trial_idx[has_spikes]\n            go = go[has_spikes]\n            edges_abs = edges_abs[has_spikes]\n            centers_abs = centers_abs[has_spikes]\n        if len(trial_idx) < 2:\n            print('%s: <2 usable trials after spike check, skipping' % sess_id, flush=True)\n            return None\n        timing['neural'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # ---------------- inputs ------------------------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        sample_start = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/sample_start_times/timestamps'][:] \\\n            if 'sample_start_times' in f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents'] else np.array([])\n        tone = tone_onset_times(trial_start, go_all, sample_start)[trial_idx]\n        time_from_tone = (centers_abs - tone[:, None]).astype(np.float32)   # (n_trials, NBINS)\n\n        stim_iv = photostim_intervals(f, trial_start, go_all)\n        photostim = np.zeros((len(trial_idx), NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n        if len(stim_iv):\n            for a, b in stim_iv:\n                # a bin is 'on' if the stimulation overlaps the bin\n                photostim[(edges_abs[:, 1:] > a) & (edges_abs[:, :-1] < b)] = 1.0\n        timing['input'] = time.time() - t0\n\n        # ---------------- outputs -----------------------------------------------------\n        t0 = time.time()\n        oc = outcome[trial_idx]\n        instr = instruction[trial_idx]\n        el_tr = early[trial_idx]\n\n        # choice: 0 no lick, 1 left, 2 right.  hit -> instructed side, miss -> other side,\n        # ignore -> no lick (identical definition to reference `behavior_report` x trial type)\n        choice = np.zeros(len(trial_idx), dtype=np.int64)\n        licked_left = ((oc == 'hit') & (instr == 'left')) | ((oc == 'miss') & (instr == 'right'))\n        licked_right = ((oc == 'hit') & (instr == 'right')) | ((oc == 'miss') & (instr == 'left'))\n        choice[licked_left] = 1\n        choice[licked_right] = 2\n\n        outcome_code = np.select([oc == 'ignore', oc == 'miss', oc == 'hit'], [0, 1, 2],\n                                 default=0).astype(np.int64)\n        early_code = (el_tr == 'early').astype(np.int64)\n\n        tongue_y, tongue_visible = tongue_y_per_bin(f, edges_abs)\n        tongue_class = discretize_tongue(tongue_y, tongue_visible)\n        timing['output'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    # ---------------- assemble ---------------------------------------------------------\n    neural = [np.ascontiguousarray(fr[:, i, :]) for i in range(fr.shape[1])]\n    inputs = [np.stack([time_from_tone[i], photostim[i]]).astype(np.float32)\n              for i in range(len(trial_idx))]\n    outputs = []\n    for i in range(len(trial_idx)):\n        o = np.empty((4, NBINS), dtype=np.int64)\n        o[0] = choice[i]\n        o[1] = outcome_code[i]\n        o[2] = early_code[i]\n        o[3] = tongue_class[i]\n        outputs.append(o)\n\n    region_names = np.array(region_names)\n    result = {\n        'session_id': sess_id,\n        'subject': subject,\n        'session_start': session_start,\n        'neural': neural,\n        'input': inputs,\n        'output': outputs,\n        'region_names': region_names,\n        'n_units_total': int(len(classification)),\n        'n_trials_file': int(ntrials_file),\n        'trial_idx': trial_idx,\n        'timing': timing,\n        'duration': time.time() - t_start,\n    }\n    if make_plots:\n        plot_processing(result, path, go, tone, edges_abs, tongue_y, tongue_visible, plot_dir)\n    print('%s: %d units, %d/%d trials, %.1fs' %\n          (sess_id, len(unit_ids), len(trial_idx), ntrials_file, result['duration']), flush=True)\n    return result\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- tongue\ndef tongue_y_per_bin(f, edges_abs):\n    \"\"\"Mean tongue y position per 50 ms bin, and whether the tongue was visible.\n\n    The side-camera DeepLabCut markers are sampled every 3.4 ms.  As in the\n    reference alignment script the marker stream is aligned to the go cue; here\n    all frames falling inside a bin are averaged (the reference used the last\n    frame of each 3.4 ms bin, which for a 3.4 ms bin is the same operation).\n    Frames with DLC likelihood <= threshold are 'tongue not visible'.\n    \"\"\"\n    bt = f['acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n    key = None\n    for k in ('Camera0_side_TongueTracking', 'Camera3_side_TongueTracking'):\n        if k in bt:\n            key = k\n            break\n    ntrials = edges_abs.shape[0]\n    if key is None:\n        return (np.full((ntrials, NBINS), np.nan), np.zeros((ntrials, NBINS), dtype=bool))\n\n    data = bt[key]['data'][:]\n    ts = bt[key]['timestamps'][:]\n    y = data[:, 1]\n    lik = data[:, 2]\n    visible = (lik > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESHOLD) & np.isfinite(y)\n\n    # cumulative sums let us average the frames inside every bin without a loop\n    yv = np.where(visible, y, 0.0)\n    cs_y = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(yv)])\n    cs_n = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(visible.astype(np.int64))])\n    idx = np.searchsorted(ts, edges_abs)                  # (ntrials, NBINS+1)\n    lo, hi = idx[:, :-1], idx[:, 1:]\n    nvis = cs_n[hi] - cs_n[lo]\n    sumy = cs_y[hi] - cs_y[lo]\n    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n        mean_y = np.where(nvis > 0, sumy / np.maximum(nvis, 1), np.nan)\n    return mean_y, nvis > 0\n\n\ndef discretize_tongue(mean_y, visible):\n    \"\"\"0: < 40th pctile, 1: 40-60th pctile, 2: > 60th pctile, 3: not visible.\n\n    Percentiles are computed per session over all bins in which the tongue is visible.\n    \"\"\"\n    cls = np.full(mean_y.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)\n    vals = mean_y[visible]\n    if vals.size == 0:\n        return cls\n    p40, p60 = np.percentile(vals, [40, 60])\n    cls[visible & (mean_y < p40)] = 0\n    cls[visible & (mean_y >= p40) & (mean_y <= p60)] = 1\n    cls[visible & (mean_y > p60)] = 2\n    return cls\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- plotting\ndef plot_processing(res, path, go, tone, edges_abs, tongue_y, tongue_visible, plot_dir):\n    \"\"\"Visualise every processing step for one session.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    sess = res['session_id']\n    neural = res['neural']\n    inputs = res['input']\n    outputs = res['output']\n    ntrials = len(neural)\n    tt = BIN_CENTERS\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(15, 14))\n\n    # 1. raw spike raster vs binned rates for one trial / few neurons\n    with h5py.File(path, 'r') as f:\n        u = f['units']\n        s0, s1 = _spike_slices(u['spike_times_index'][:])\n        classification = u['classification'][:]\n        anno = _decode(u['anno_name'][:])\n        good = np.where((classification == b'good') & np.array([a.strip() != '' for a in anno]))[0]\n        tr = min(5, ntrials - 1)\n        show = good[:30]\n        spikes_all = u['spike_times']\n        for j, k in enumerate(show):\n            sp = spikes_all[s0[k]:s1[k]]\n            sp = sp[(sp >= edges_abs[tr, 0]) & (sp < edges_abs[tr, -1])] - go[tr]\n            ax[0, 0].plot(sp, np.full(len(sp), j), '|', color='k', ms=4)\n    ax[0, 0].set_title('%s trial %d: raw spikes (aligned to go cue)' % (sess, tr))\n    ax[0, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax[0, 0].set_ylabel('neuron')\n    ax[0, 0].set_xlim(OFF_START, OFF_END)\n    ax[0, 0].axvline(0, color='r')\n\n    im = ax[0, 1].imshow(neural[tr][:30], aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n                         extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, -0.5, min(30, len(show)) - 0.5])\n    ax[0, 1].set_title('binned firing rate (Hz), same trial/neurons')\n    ax[0, 1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax[0, 1])\n\n    # 2. population PSTH sorted by choice\n    ch = np.array([o[0, 0] for o in outputs])\n    for c, lab, col in [(1, 'lick left', 'b'), (2, 'lick right', 'r'), (0, 'no lick', 'gray')]:\n        m = np.where(ch == c)[0]\n        if len(m) == 0:\n            continue\n        psth = np.mean([neural[i].mean(axis=0) for i in m], axis=0)\n        ax[1, 0].plot(tt, psth, col, label='%s (n=%d)' % (lab, len(m)))\n    ax[1, 0].axvline(0, color='k', ls='--')\n    ax[1, 0].set_title('population mean rate by choice')\n    ax[1, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax[1, 0].set_ylabel('Hz')\n    ax[1, 0].legend()\n\n    # 3. inputs\n    for i in range(min(8, ntrials)):\n        ax[1, 1].plot(tt, inputs[i][0], alpha=0.7)\n    ax[1, 1].axvline(0, color='k', ls='--')\n    ax[1, 1].set_title('input 0: time from tone onset (s), 8 trials')\n    ax[1, 1].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    stim = np.array([inp[1] for inp in inputs])\n    im = ax[2, 0].imshow(stim, aspect='auto', origin='lower', interpolation='nearest',\n                         extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, ntrials])\n    ax[2, 0].set_title('input 1: photostim on (%d stim trials)' % int((stim.max(axis=1) > 0).sum()))\n    ax[2, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax[2, 0].set_ylabel('trial')\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax[2, 0])\n\n    # 4. tongue: continuous y and discretisation\n    vals = tongue_y[tongue_visible]\n    if vals.size:\n        p40, p60 = np.percentile(vals, [40, 60])\n        ax[2, 1].hist(vals, bins=60)\n        ax[2, 1].axvline(p40, color='r', label='40th pctile')\n        ax[2, 1].axvline(p60, color='g', label='60th pctile')\n        ax[2, 1].legend()\n    ax[2, 1].set_title('tongue y over visible bins (session percentiles)')\n\n    tcls = np.array([o[3] for o in outputs])\n    im = ax[3, 0].imshow(tcls, aspect='auto', origin='lower', interpolation='nearest',\n                         extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, ntrials], vmin=0, vmax=3)\n    ax[3, 0].set_title('output 3: tongue y class (3 = not visible)')\n    ax[3, 0].set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax[3, 0].set_ylabel('trial')\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax[3, 0])\n\n    # 5. per-trial outputs\n    oc = np.array([o[1, 0] for o in outputs])\n    ea = np.array([o[2, 0] for o in outputs])\n    ax[3, 1].plot(ch, '.', label='choice (0 none,1 L,2 R)')\n    ax[3, 1].plot(oc + 0.1, '.', label='outcome (0 ig,1 miss,2 hit)')\n    ax[3, 1].plot(ea + 0.2, '.', label='early lick')\n    ax[3, 1].set_xlabel('trial')\n    ax[3, 1].legend(fontsize=7)\n    ax[3, 1].set_title('per-trial outputs')\n\n    fig.suptitle('Processing summary %s' % sess)\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    out = os.path.join(plot_dir, 'processing_%s.png' % sess)\n    fig.savefig(out, dpi=110)\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print('  wrote', out, flush=True)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- main\ndef _worker(args):\n    path, make_plots = args\n    try:\n        return process_session(path, make_plots=make_plots)\n    except Exception as exc:                                   # keep going on bad files\n        import traceback\n        print('ERROR processing %s: %s' % (path, exc), flush=True)\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str)\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n                    help='save processing plots for up to 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=16)\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'sub-*', '*.nwb')))\n    if args.sample:\n        files = files[:2]\n    print('Processing %d sessions with %d workers' % (len(files), args.nproc), flush=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    plot_flags = [args.show_processing and i < 2 for i in range(len(files))]\n    if args.nproc > 1 and len(files) > 1:\n        with Pool(min(args.nproc, len(files))) as pool:\n            results = pool.map(_worker, list(zip(files, plot_flags)), chunksize=1)\n    else:\n        results = [_worker(a) for a in zip(files, plot_flags)]\n    results = [r for r in results if r is not None]\n    print('Processed %d sessions in %.1f s (%.2f s/session)'\n          % (len(results), time.time() - t0, (time.time() - t0) / max(len(results), 1)), flush=True)\n\n    # reward not driven by the animal's choice, and the reference `get_regular_trial_mask` also drops them). Early-lick, ignore/miss and photostim trials are **kept** because the decoder must predict early lick and outcome and receives photostim as an input; dropping them would make three of the four outputs degenerate.\n230:3. **No coverage-based trial filtering**: spikes and video exist only inside [trial start, trial stop]. Error (miss) trials are terminated ~0.8 s after the go cue, so requiring the whole [-2.5, +1.5] s window to be observed would delete 95% of miss trials. Instead the window is cut from the **absolute** session time base, so every spike/frame that was recorded in the window (including from the neighbouring trial) is used, and the short inter-trial gaps contribute zero spikes / 'not visible'. Worst-case coverage is 66% of the window, mean ~97%.\n232:5. **Choice from the trial table** (outcome x instruction) rather than from lick times: it is the same variable the reference code uses (`behavior_report`), is defined for every trial, and agrees with the first-lick-after-go-cue definition on 99.4% of trials.\n233:6. **Tongue visibility threshold 0.9** on the DLC likelihood: the likelihood distribution is strongly bimodal (99.8% of frames are <0.1 or >0.9), so the exact threshold is irrelevant.\n234:7. **Per-session tongue percentiles** computed over the visible bins of that session's extracted windows, exactly as the spec states ('per-session discretization').\n236:9. **All 173 sessions with >=1 good unit are kept** (the 174th has no good units). No behavioral-performance session filter is applied: the DANDI release already contains the sessions the authors published, and dropping low-performance sessions would remove error trials needed for the outcome output.\n241:- [x] Behavioral performance ~84%, trials/session ~476-546.\n242:- [ ] Independent re-computation of binned rates for a random (session, trial, neuron) directly from the NWB file (`np.allclose`).\n243:- [ ] Independent re-computation of the photostim input and the time-from-tone input for random trials.\n244:- [ ] Independent re-computation of the tongue class for random trials, and check the class frequencies are ~40/20/40 among visible bins.\n245:- [ ] Choice/outcome/early-lick distributions in the converted data match the raw trial-table distributions.\n246:- [ ] Per-trial neural array shape (n_neurons, 80) and constant n_neurons within a session.\n260:   * selects trials: drops `auto_water`/`free_water` trials (reference `get_regular_trial_mask`),\n261:     trials outside the units' `obs_intervals`, and trials with no recorded spikes at all,\n266:   * builds the outputs (choice, outcome, early lick, discretized tongue y).\n269:   the same trial, population PSTH split by choice, the two inputs, the tongue-y histogram with the\n270:   40th/60th percentile cuts, the tongue class raster and the per-trial outputs.\n273:- Reading `units/spike_times` per unit from HDF5 is slow -> the whole ragged array is read once and\n274:  sliced with the index vector.\n275:- Binning with nested Python loops (as in the reference `sliding_histogram`) is O(n_bins x n_trials)\n276:  per unit -> replaced by one `np.searchsorted` per unit over all bin edges (vectorised over trials).\n283:1. **All-zero trials**: in 8 of 173 sessions the ephys recording covers only a contiguous subset of the\n284:   behavioural trials. No further firing-rate threshold is applied: the method paper's 2-Hz cut was specific to its ridge-regression encoding analysis, would remove 33% of the units, and the decoder benefits from all recorded units.\n2. **Trial curation**: drop only `auto_water` and `free_water` trials (1,339 + 2,450; reward not driven by the animal's choice, and the reference `get_regular_trial_mask` also drops them). Early-lick, ignore/miss and photostim trials are **kept** because the decoder must predict early lick and outcome and receives photostim as an input; dropping them would make three of the four outputs degenerate.\n3. **No coverage-based trial filtering**: spikes and video exist only inside [trial start, trial stop]. Frames with `likelihood < 0.5` are set to NaN, since the tracker still reports a position when the tongue is not protruding. The surviving values are averaged into 50 ms bins across the whole session, and the 40th and 60th percentiles of *those bin means* give two class edges. Each trial's window is then binned the same way and digitised against those edges, giving classes 0/1/2. Bins containing no visible frame are assigned a fourth class, `3` (`'not visible'`).\n\nii.\n```python\nTONGUE_CONF = 0.5                # tracking likelihood for the tongue to count as visible\nTONGUE_PCT = (40, 60)            # per-session percentiles -> 3 visible classes\nTONGUE_HIDDEN = 3                # no visible tongue frame in the bin\n```\n\n```python\ny[data[:, 2] < TONGUE_CONF] = np.nan     # keep only visible frames\n\n# bin the whole session on one grid, then take percentiles of the bin means\ngidx = np.floor((t_cam - t_cam[0]) / BIN).astype(np.int64)\nedges = np.nanpercentile(_bin_mean(gidx, y, int(gidx[-1]) + 1), TONGUE_PCT)\n\n# per trial, bin the same way and digitise against those edges\ncls = np.full((len(go), N_BINS), TONGUE_HIDDEN, np.int8)\nlo = np.searchsorted(t_cam, go + T_START, 'left')\nhi = np.searchsorted(t_cam, go + T_STOP, 'left')\nfor i in range(len(go)):\n    b = np.floor((t_cam[lo[i]:hi[i]] - (go[i] + T_START)) / BIN).astype(np.int64)\n    np.clip(b, 0, N_BINS - 1, out=b)\n    m = _bin_mean(b, y[lo[i]:hi[i]], N_BINS)\n    ok = ~np.isnan(m)\n    cls[i, ok] = np.digitize(m[ok], edges)\n```\n\niii. The tongue is visible in only ~10% of frames, and when it is retracted the tracker still emits a position, so those frames are discarded rather than averaged in — including them shifts the percentiles and mixes real protrusions with noise. The 40th/60th split and the per-session scope both follow the instructions. A fourth class is defined for bins with no visible tongue, which is 75% of all bins.\n\n## 8-d. How is `output` *tongue_y_position* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. This is the one genuinely time-varying output, so it is the only one needing real alignment. The camera timestamps are on the same session-absolute clock as the spikes and the go cues, so each trial's frame range is found by `searchsorted` on the camera timestamps at `go + T_START` and `go + T_STOP`, and frames are assigned to bins by their offset from `go + T_START` — the same go-cue-relative grid used for the firing rates.\n\nii.\n```python\nlo = np.searchsorted(t_cam, go + T_START, 'left')\nhi = np.searchsorted(t_cam, go + T_STOP, 'left')\nfor i in range(len(go)):\n    b = np.floor((t_cam[lo[i]:hi[i]] - (go[i] + T_START)) / BIN).astype(np.int64)\n```\n\niii. The camera timestamps share the global clock with the spikes and events, so no interpolation or offset correction is needed — the same bin grid is applied to both streams, which guarantees bin `k` of the tongue output covers the same interval as bin `k` of the firing rates.\n\nUnlike the spikes, the video is trial-gated: it runs from `trials.start_time` to the trial-end event and is off during the inter-trial interval. On the ~3% of trials where the go cue falls less than 2.5 s after trial start, the leading bins therefore contain no frames at all and fall into the `'not visible'` class.\n\n## 9. Missing data is handled one of two ways depending on what it means. Where nothing was recorded, the session or trial is excluded, since emitting it would fabricate data — a trial with no spikes would otherwise appear as 4 s of 0 Hz across every unit. Where the measurement legitimately has no value, as with a retracted tongue, it is represented as an explicit category rather than imputed.\n\n## 10-a. The tongue loop could be vectorised with a global bin index and one `bincount`, but it runs over trials rather than frames and is not a measurable share of runtime, so it was left in the clearer form.\n\n## 10-c. How is memory usage optimized?\n\ni. Firing rates are stored as `float32` and the outputs as `int8` rather than the default `float64`. Each session is read inside a `with` block so the file handle and its cached arrays are released on exit, and only the per-trial slices are retained. The full neural payload is 11.9 GB; a single session's working arrays are at most ~0.2 GB.\n\nii.\n```python\nrates = np.empty((good.size, n_trials, N_BINS), np.float32)\n```\n\n```python\nout = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n```\n\n```python\ninp = np.stack([time_from_tone, photostim], axis=1).astype(np.float32)\n```\n\niii. `float32` halves the largest object in the output relative to `float64` at no cost, since firing rates are multiples of 20 Hz and well within its precision.
